def adam_optimize(h0,n,alpha_start,alpha_end,iters,deadline,seed,lr0,lr_end,best_c):
  h=feasible copy; best_c=compute_exact; best_h copy.
  m=np.zeros(n); v=...
  for it:
    if time>deadline-break
    F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha_current)
    exact=r.max(); update best.
    # adapt alpha: linearly increase? α start 100 end 2000.
    t=1; m=...; step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
    h_new=project_fast(h - step)
    # optional if exact worsens by >0.001 for many, reduce lr? Keep line search?
    h=h_new
  return best.

Pseudo code:
def adam_optimize(h0, n, deadline, seed, alpha_lo=50, alpha_hi=5000, lr0=0.1, lr1=0.005, max_iter=None):
  h=project_feasible_fast(h0,n)
  best_c=compute_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # sometimes randomize alpha? 
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
  t=0; n_iter estimate maybe deadline-t0? Just loop while time<deadline.
  stall=0
  while time.time() < deadline:
    alpha = alpha_lo + (alpha_hi-alpha_lo)*min(1, t/2000)
    F,r,weights,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
    exact = float(r.max())
    if exact < best_c - 1e-14:
       best_c=exact; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
    else:
       stall +=1
    t +=1
    # adaptive lr
    lr = lr0 * (lr1/lr0)**(min(1,t/1000))
    if stall > 100:
       lr *= 0.5? maybe too slow; also alpha lower to explore.
    m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
    mhat=m/(1-0.9**t); vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
    step = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    h_new = project_fast(h - step, n/2)
    # optional line search: if exact_new > exact + 0.02, reduce lr (store)
    exact_new = compute_exact(h_new,n) # computes r full; already might need F? We only have exact. If bad, skip F1.
    if exact_new > exact + 0.05:
       lr *= 0.7
       # maybe reduce step by normalizing: h = project_fast(h - lr? But we already have step. Use h unchanged.
    else:
       h=h_new
  return best_h,best_c

But compute_exact each iteration duplicates r computed in smooth_obj_grad. We can return r and exact. Use F,r,weights,grad; exact = r.max(). For line search exact_new, need r_new maybe not gradient; compute_r only O(n^2), cheaper than full smooth? Use compute_r(h_new,n).max.

def pair_delta(h,n,i,j,si):
  L=2n-1; darr=np.zeros(L)
  add_single(darr,i,si,h,n)
  add_single(darr,j,-si,h,n)
  if i!=j:
    d=abs(i-j); corr = -si*(-si)? product = -si^2; -product=si^2.
    darr[n-1+d] += si*si
    darr[n-1-d] += si*si
  return darr

Our add_single linear: i Δδ: first at k0 δ(1-a); second -δ a => δ -2aδ. j Δ=-δ: first -δ(1-b); second +δ b => -δ +2bδ. Sum linear 2(b-a)δ; zero corrections -2δ^2. Correct. Good.

Project fast details:
def project_feasible_fast(h, n):
 target = 0.5*n
 h = np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff = target - h.sum()
 if diff>0:
   room=np.maximum(0,1-h); tot=room.sum()
   if tot>1e-15: h = h + diff*room/tot
 else if diff<0:
   tot=h.sum()
   if tot>1e-15: h = h + diff*h/tot
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # small correction loop up to 5
 for _ in range(5):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot>0: h += diff*room/tot
   else:
      tot=h.sum(); if tot>0: h += diff*h/tot
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
This is not exact projection but okay. For initial, maybe use Euclidean projection with bisection to be exact. We can use same; after bisection not needed. But to be feasible with exact sum, this returns sum target within 1e-15.

Let's detail weighted_gradient:
def weighted_gradient(weights,h,n):
    L=2*n-1
    w0=weights[n-1]
    if n==1: return np.zeros(1) # n always >1
    pos=weights[n:]  # k=1..n-1
    neg=weights[:n-1][::-1]  # d=1..n-1
    # const
    # suffix_pos: use cumsum pos
    cpos = np.empty(n)
    cpos[n-1]=0
    # Could do vector:
    if n>1:
       cpos_arr = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos)))  # length n
       suffix_pos = cpos_arr[n-1 - np.arange(n)]
    else zeros
    cneg_arr = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(neg)))
    prefix_neg = cneg_arr[np.arange(n)]
    const = w0 + suffix_pos + prefix_neg
    wsum = np.zeros(n)
    wsum[1:] = pos + neg
    # convolve
    direct = np.convolve(wsum, h, mode='full')
    rev = np.convolve(wsum, h[::-1], mode='full')
    term_past = direct[:n]
    term_future = rev[:n][::-1]
    grad_unnorm = const - 2.0*w0*h - term_past - term_future
    dx=2.0/n
    return dx * grad_unnorm
This creates arrays. For n small okay.
Potential issue if weights all zeros? no.

Can optimize by using FFT if n>800? Not needed maybe n<=512. But maybe choose n=768? Use np.convolve direct O(n^2) maybe 1M; okay. If n=1024, 4M per grad (two conv plus correlate) maybe 500k? Actually direct convolve n=1024 1M ops, ~0.001? Fine. We can choose n=512.

Smooth obj:
def smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha):
    r = compute_r_from_h(h,n)
    # For LSE, use r positive
    m = float(r.max())
    scaled = alpha * (r - m)
    # exp underflow okay. If alpha very large, only active. Ensure sum not 0: use max=1.
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp = float(exps.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <=0:
       weights = np.zeros(2*n-1); weights[int(np.argmax(r))]=1/ (sum?) Actually if all zero? set uniform? 
    weights = exps / sumexp
    F = (m + np.log(sumexp)) / alpha if alpha>0 else m
    grad = weighted_gradient(weights,h,n)
    return F, r, weights, grad
If alpha=0? always >0.

Compute_r:
def compute_r_from_h(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    P = np.correlate(h,h,'full')
    L=2*n-1
    r=np.empty(L)
    total = float(h.sum())
    prefix=np.cumsum(h)
    r[n-1]=dx*(total - P[n-1])
    if n>1:
       d=np.arange(1,n)
       m = n - d
       # pos_linear prefix[m-1]; m arrays
       pos_lin = prefix[m-1]
       neg_lin = total - prefix[d-1]
       pd = P[n-1 + d]  # length n-1
       r[n-1 + d] = dx*(pos_lin - pd)
       r[n-1 - d] = dx*(neg_lin - pd)
    r = np.clip(r, 0.0, None)
    return r
Potential: np.cumsum returns length n; prefix[d-1] ok. P[n-1-d] same as pd but due symmetry use pd.
Clipping may affect if true c negative due floating; fine.

Sym project:
def sym_proj(h,n):
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h,n)

Interpolate:
def interp_h(hsrc,nsrc,ndst):
    if nsrc==ndst: return hsrc.copy()
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nsrc,endpoint=False)
    xd=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,ndst,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(xd,xs,hsrc)

Adam:
def adam_optimize(h0,n,deadline,seed,use_sym=False,alpha_lo=80.0,alpha_hi=4000.0,lr0=0.06,lr1=0.002):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
   best_c=compute_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
   t=0; base_lr=lr0
   min_time = max(2.0, min(20.0, (deadline - time.time())*0.5)) # But loop uses deadline absolute.
   while time.time() < deadline:
       # alpha = alpha_lo + (alpha_hi-alpha_lo)* (t/(t+500))? use logistic
       frac = min(1.0, t/800.0)
       alpha = alpha_lo + (alpha_hi-alpha_lo)*frac
       F,r,weights,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
       exact = float(r.max())
       if exact < best_c - 1e-13:
           best_c = exact; best_h = h.copy()
       t += 1
       lr = lr0 * (lr1/lr0)**(min(1.0, t/1500.0))
       # if exact stuck for long, random perturbation?
       # Update moments
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
       v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(grad*grad)
       bias1 = 1 - 0.9**t
       bias2 = 1 - 0.999**t
       step = lr * (m/bias1) / (np.sqrt(v/bias2)+1e-8)
       # limit step norm
       snorm = np.linalg.norm(step)
       if snorm > 0.1:
           step *= 0.1/snorm
       if use_sym:
           # project step onto symmetric? h_new = h - step; but sym project after.
           h_new = sym_proj(h - step, n)
       else:
           h_new = project_feasible(h - step, n)
       # occasional exact recompute to guard round? no.
       h = h_new
   # final exact
   best_c = compute_exact(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c
Potential issue: r computed with clip; exact used for best maybe r.max. But final exact maybe different? Use compute_exact at update? That doubles cost. Instead update best with r.max, and final recompute. Candidate best_h may have r.max lower than exact? Formula should match. Good.

But if use_sym, gradient not sym. We should sym gradients before Adam: if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1]). Do after smooth_obj_grad.

Could include a small random perturbation every 200 iters if no improvement: if t % 200 ==0 and no_best_recent: h=sym_proj(h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n),n). Track best_since.
Implement stall counter: if exact < best_c+1e-12: stall=0 else stall+=1; if stall>300: h=sym_proj(h + rng.normal(0, 0.05/n? maybe 0.02,n)); m=v=0? lr reset? Keep best.
But random perturb may make worse; okay.

Pair polish:
def polish_pairs(h0,n,deadline,seed,alpha=1000.0):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')  # unnormalized
   dx=2.0/n; best_m=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   L=2*n-1
   # get grad for sign
   F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
   # sort indices by grad
   top_idx = np.where(h<0.999)[0]? We choose random from quantiles.
   # Precompute frames? no.
   accepted=0
   last_grad_update=0
   while time.time() < deadline:
      if accepted > 50 or t_count % 100 ==0: recompute grad? maybe expensive. Use time.
      if t_count % 50 ==0:
         F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
         exact = r.max()
         if exact < best_m -... update? But exact r max in normalized; best_m unnormalized; compare best_m * dx.
      # choose pair based on grad:
      # sample i from high grad if diff>0? We'll sample random two and compute sign.
      i = rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      if i==j: continue
      diff = grad[i] - grad[j]
      if diff ==0: continue
      mag_base = 0.05
      # if diff>0, s negative; else positive
      sign = -1.0 if diff>0 else 1.0
      # max allowed positive/negative
      if sign>0:
         max_mag = min(1.0-h[i], h[j], mag_base)
         if max_mag<=1e-8: continue
      else:
         max_mag = min(h[i], 1.0-h[j], mag_base)
         if max_mag<=1e-8: continue
      for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
         s = sign*max_mag*mult
         delta = pair_delta(h,n,i,j,s)
         m_new = (c + delta).max()
         if m_new < best_m - 1e-13:
             c = c + delta
             h[i] += s; h[j] -= s
             best_m = float(m_new)
             best_h = h.copy()
             accepted +=1
             # recompute grad? break out to get new grad
             break
      t_count +=1
   # final project exact
   h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
   c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); best_m=c.max()*dx
   return best_h,best_m

Pair_delta function:
def pair_delta(h,n,i,j,s):
    L=2*n-1
    darr=np.zeros(L)
    add_single_delta(darr,i,s,h,n)
    add_single_delta(darr,j,-s,h,n)
    if i != j:
       d=abs(i-j)
       corr=s*s
       darr[n-1+d] += corr
       darr[n-1-d] += corr
    return darr
add_single_delta as above. It modifies zero idx twice; if i and j same? not. Good.
For fractional, add_single_delta linear formulas with h. For k0 correction subtract delta^2. In pair delta, if joint edge k0? d>0 for different.

Could there be double correction for joint edges when i,j are in both positive/negative lags. We add corr to n-1±d. Good.

Potential issue: For fixed pair, when trying multiple magnitudes after a failed larger, we recompute delta from current h (same) each time. Fine.

Is pair polish worth? It may find local improvements after Adam. Use for best at end for n up to maybe 512. Time 30-60s. Use after SLSQP? We can run before/after.

SLSQP helpers:
def _sum_con(x,n): return float(x.sum() - 0.5*n)
def _sum_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n)
def _slsqp_obj(x,n,alpha): return float(smooth_obj_grad(x,n,alpha)[0])
def _slsqp_jac(x,n,alpha): return smooth_obj_grad(x,n,alpha)[3]

Use alpha=2000, maxiter=min(200, ...). Bounds list large; for n=512 memory okay.
def slsqp_polish(h,n,budget_s,alpha=2000,maxiter=200):
   if budget_s<20: return
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try:
      res = minimize(_slsqp_obj, h, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_slsqp_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      if hasattr(res,'x'):
         hp=project_feasible(res.x,n)
         cp=compute_exact(hp,n)
         if cp < compute_exact(h,n): return hp,cp
   except Exception: pass
   return h, compute_exact(h,n)
No kwargs kwargs. _slsqp_jac signature (x,n,alpha); SLSQP will call with x and args. Good.

Could SLSQP call with x as list? numpy ok.

Function frac_sa(h0,n,deadline,seed, use_sym=False):

  T=0.01 (normalized? use unnormalized M values; difference scaled by 1/dx? c_unnorm ~50, differences maybe small. Use T_unnorm=1.0 maybe)

    i,j random; s = rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1) times bound; ensure bounds.
    delta=pair_delta; c_new=c+delta; m_new=c_new.max()
    if m_new < best_m: accept and update best; T*=0.999
    else if rng.random() < exp(-(m_new-best_m_best? or current_m?)/T): accept current (even if worse than best)
    else reject
  Return best.

def frac_anneal(h0,n,deadline,seed,use_sym=False):
  h=project if sym
  c=correlate; current_m=c.max(); best_m=current_m; best_h=h.copy()
  T=1.0
  rng=...
  stagnant=0
  while time<deadline:
    # propose move
    if use_sym: move symmetric? Could do pair i,j and mirrored pair? Maybe too complex. Use free.
    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i==j continue
    # sample s magnitude
    # choose direction based on objective subgradient? Use random sign; magnitude exponential decay.
    mag = 0.05 * (0.5 + rng.random()) maybe; or step schedule.
    # Use if move improves? Compute active? no.
    # Ensure bounds by scaling.
    sign = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
    s=sign*mag
    if s>0: s=min(s,1-h[i],h[j]); if <=0 continue
    else: s=max(s,-h[i],? for s<0, h[i]+s>=0, h[j]-s<=1 => s>=-min(h[i],1-h[j]); set s=max(s, -min(h[i],1-h[j])))
    delta=pair_delta(...)
    c_trial=c+delta; m_trial=float(c_trial.max())
    delta_m = m_trial - current_m
    if m_trial < current_m:
       accept; T=max(T*0.999,0.01); stagnant=0
    else:
       accept_prob = np.exp(-delta_m/T) if delta_m/T < 500 else 0
       if rng.random() < accept_prob:
          accept; stagnant+=1
       else:
          stagnant+=1
    if accept: h[i]+=s; h[j]-=s; c=c_trial; current_m=m_trial; if current_m<best_m: best... 
    if stagnant > 1000:
       # reset to best and perturb
       h=best_h.copy(); c=correlate(h); current_m=best_m
       # random warm
       T=1.0
       stagnant=0
  return best_h, best_m*dx

That would convolve each, not good. Need formula per lag O(n). Let's derive gradient contribution for a single lag k (±d or 0). From earlier direct c_k gradient:
If k=0: dx*w*(1-2h).
If k>0: For i=0..n-k-1: grad[i] += dx*w*(1-h[i+k]); grad[i+k] += dx*w*(-h[i]).
If k<0 (d=-k): i=d..n-1 first: grad[i] += dx*w*(1-h[i-d]); i=d? second: for i=d..n-1? As second j=i-d: grad[j] -= dx*w*h[i]? Let's write direct loops vectorized slices:
For k>0 d:
 grad[0:n-d] += dx*w*(1 - h[d:n])
 grad[d:n] += dx*w*(-h[0:n-d])
For k<0 -d:
 edges first i=d..n-1 with q=i-d: grad[d:n] += dx*w*(1 - h[0:n-d])
 second j=0..n-d-1? p=j+d=i; p index d..n-1, second j=0..n-d-1: grad[0:n-d] += dx*w*(-h[d:n])
Thus for d>0, contribution for lag +d:
 g[:n-d] += w*dx*(1 - h[d:])
 g[d:] += -w*dx*h[:n-d]
For lag -d:
 g[d:] += w*dx*(1 - h[:n-d])
 g[:n-d] += -w*dx*h[d:]
This is O(n) per lag but vectorized. For K=10, 10 vector ops length ~n, faster than conv.

Need add_single_lag_grad top-level:
def add_lag_grad(grad, k, w, h, n):
   if w ==0: return
   dx=2.0/n
   if k==0:
      grad += w*dx*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
   elif k>0:
      d=k; m=n-d
      cm=w*dx
      grad[:m] += cm*(1.0 - h[d:])
      grad[d:] -= cm*h[:m]
   else:
      d=-k; m=n+d
      cm=w*dx
      grad[d:] += cm*(1.0 - h[:m])
      grad[:m] -= cm*h[d:]
Check for k<0: Use earlier: r_{-d}=∑_{i=d}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-d}). grad first (i): grad[d:] += w(1-h[:m]); second (j=i-d): grad[:m] += -w*h[d:]. yes.
This sparse gradient should match full within tolerance. For LSE weights sum 1. Good.
This can speed high α dramatically. At α=1000, maybe top_count 5. At α=80, top_count maybe >32, full.

def random_smooth_start(n,rng,variant=0,use_sym=False):
 x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
 h=np.full(n,0.5)
 K=5
 for m in range(1,K+1):
    amp = 0.35 / (m**1.2) * (0.3+0.7*rng.rand())
    ph = 2*np.pi*rng.rand()
    # sign for sin/cos
    h = h + amp*np.sin(np.pi*m*x + ph)
    h = h + 0.5*amp*np.cos(np.pi*m*x + 2*np.pi*rng.rand())
 # add a random low hump
 if variant%2==0:
    mu=0.8+0.4*rng.rand(); sig=0.3+0.5*rng.rand(); amp=0.5*rng.rand()
    h += amp*np.exp(-((x-mu)**2)/(2*sig*sig))
 h = project_feasible(h,n)
 if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
 return h

def frac_anneal(h0,n,deadline,seed,use_sym=False, T0=2.0):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n); if use_sym sym
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   current=float(c.max()); best=current; best_h=h.copy()
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   T=T0
   grad=None
   step=0
   since_best=0
   def? no closures. Need top-level update. We'll code inline.
   grad,_,_,weights? We can call smooth_obj_grad(get grad). But compute alpha=1000.
   F,r,weights,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0, allow_sparse=True)
   if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
   min_T=1e-4
   while time.time()<deadline:
      # propose
      i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      if i==j: continue
      if rng.random()<0.75 and grad is not None:
         diff=grad[i]-grad[j]
         if diff>1e-14: sign=-1.0
         elif diff<-1e-14: sign=1.0
         else: sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
      else:
         sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
      # magnitude: start with 0.02, random exponent
      mag=0.01 + 0.05*rng.rand()
      s=sign*mag
      if s>0:
         maxs=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
         s=min(s,maxs)
      else:
         maxs=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
         s=max(s, -maxs)  # s negative
      if abs(s)<1e-8: continue
      delta=pair_delta(h,n,i,j,s)
      c_trial=c+delta
      m_trial=float(c_trial.max())
      dE=m_trial-current
      accept=False
      if dE <= 0:
          accept=True
          T=max(0.995*T, 1e-4)
      else:
          if dE < 50*T:
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-dE/T): accept=True
      if accept:
          h[i]+=s; h[j]-=s; c=c_trial; current=m_trial
          since_best +=1
          if current < best - 1e-14:
             best=current; best_h=h.copy(); since_best=0
          step +=1
          if step % 50 ==0:
             # recompute grad occasionally
             F,r,weights,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0, True)
             if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
          if step % 1000 ==0:
             # recompute c exact? maybe if drift
             c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
             current=float(c.max())
          if since_best > 2000:
             h=best_h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); current=best; since_best=0; T=T0
             F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0,True)
      else:
          # no update; step? count proposals maybe
          pass
   # final project and exact
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
   best_c=compute_exact(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c

Now, compute_exact:
def compute_exact(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   dx=2.0/n
   return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)

Now, SLSQP polish function:
def slsqp_polish(h0,n,budget_s,alpha=2000.0):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   c0=compute_exact(h,n)
   if budget_s < 15: return h,c0
   maxiter = 80 if n>300 else 150
   if n>500: maxiter=40
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try:
      res = minimize(_slsqp_obj, h, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_slsqp_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      if hasattr(res,'x'):
         hp=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,float),n)
         cp=compute_exact(hp,n)
         if cp < c0: return hp,cp
   except Exception:
      pass
   return h,c0

Code skeleton:
```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(10):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def sym_proj(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h,n)
...
```
Project loop: h = h + ... creates new; clip. Good.

compute_r:
```
def compute_r(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    dx=2.0/n
    P=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')
    L=2*n-1
    r=np.zeros(L,dtype=float)
    total=float(h.sum())
    if n==1:
       r[0]=dx*(total - P[0])
       return np.maximum(r,0.0)
    prefix=np.cumsum(h)
    r[n-1]=dx*(total - P[n-1])
    d=np.arange(1,n,dtype=int)
    m=n-d
    pos_lin=prefix[m-1]
    neg_lin=total - prefix[d-1]
    pd=P[n-1+d]
    r[n-1+d]=dx*(pos_lin - pd)
    r[n-1-d]=dx*(neg_lin - pd)
    return np.maximum(r,0.0)
```
For n=1, n-1=0, P[0]=h^2. d loop none.

compute_exact:
```
def compute_exact(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)
```

interpolate:
```
def interp_h(hsrc,nsrc,ndst):
    if nsrc==ndst: return np.array(hsrc,dtype=float).copy()
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nsrc,endpoint=False)
    xd=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,ndst,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(xd,xs,np.asarray(hsrc,float))
```

add_lag_grad:
```
def add_lag_grad(grad,k,w,h,n):
    if w==0.0: return
    dx=2.0/n
    cm=w*dx
    if k==0:
       grad += cm*(1.0-2.0*h)
    elif k>0:
       d=k; m=n-d
       if m>0:
          grad[:m] += cm*(1.0 - h[d:])
          grad[d:] -= cm*h[:m]
    else:
       d=-k; m=n+d
       if m>0:
          grad[d:] += cm*(1.0 - h[:m])
          grad[:m] -= cm*h[d:]
```
For k<0, h[:m] length m=n-d, h[d:] length m. Good.

weighted_gradient:
```
def weighted_gradient(weights,h,n,allow_sparse=True):
    if n==1:
       return np.zeros(1)
    if allow_sparse:
       idx=np.argsort(weights)[::-1]
       cum=0.0
       top=0
       for ii in idx:
          w=weights[ii]
          if w <= 1e-12: break
          top +=1
          cum += w
          if cum >= 0.999:
             break
          if top >=32:
             break
       if top <=32 and cum >=0.99:
          grad=np.zeros(n)
          for t in range(top):
             ii=int(idx[t])
             add_lag_grad(grad, ii-(n-1), weights[ii], h, n)
          return grad
    w0=weights[n-1]
    pos=weights[n:]
    neg=weights[:n-1][::-1]
    # suffix
    ar=np.arange(n)
    if n>1:
      cpos=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos)))
      suffix_pos=cpos[n-1-ar]
      cneg=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(neg)))
      prefix_neg=cneg[ar]
    else:
      suffix_pos=np.zeros(1); prefix_neg=np.zeros(1)
    const=w0 + suffix_pos + prefix_neg
    wsum=np.zeros(n)
    wsum[1:]=pos+neg
    direct=np.convolve(wsum,h,mode='full')
    rev=np.convolve(wsum,h[::-1],mode='full')
    term_past=direct[:n]
    term_future=rev[:n][::-1]
    grad_un=const - 2.0*w0*h - term_past - term_future
    return (2.0/n)*grad_un
```
If n=1, pos and neg empty; but n always >1 maybe.
In sparse top, top initialized 0; if first w small? weights max positive. If cumulative <0.99 after 32, full.

smooth_obj_grad:
```
def smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha,allow_sparse=True):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if alpha <=0: alpha=1.0
    r=compute_r(h,n)
    m=float(r.max())
    scaled=alpha*(r-m)
    # numeric safety
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp=float(exps.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <=0:
        exps=np.zeros_like(r)
        exps[int(np.argmax(r))]=1.0
        sumexp=1.0
    weights=exps/sumexp
    F=(m + math.log(sumexp))/alpha
    grad=weighted_gradient(weights,h,n,allow_sparse)
    return F,r,weights,grad
```
Since scaled max 0, no overflow. If m=0 and all r=0, sumexp=L, weights uniform; F=0. But sum target makes c0 positive maybe.

Adam:
```
def adam_optimize(h0,n,deadline,seed,use_sym=False,alpha_lo=80.0,alpha_hi=4000.0,lr0=0.06,lr1=0.002):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
   best_c=compute_exact(h,n)
   best_h=h.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
   t=0; stall=0
   clip=0.1
   while time.time() < deadline:
       frac=min(1.0, t/800.0)
       alpha=alpha_lo + (alpha_hi-alpha_lo)*frac
       F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha, True)
       if use_sym:
          grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
       exact=float(r.max())
       if exact < best_c - 1e-13:
          best_c=exact; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
       else:
          stall +=1
       t +=1
       lr = lr0 * (lr1/lr0)**(min(1.0, t/1500.0))
       if stall > 400:
          h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
          if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
          else: h=project_feasible(h,n)
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); stall=0
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
       v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(grad*grad)
       b1=1.0-0.9**t
       b2=1.0-0.999**t
       step = lr * (m/b1) / (np.sqrt(v/b2)+1e-8)
       step = np.clip(step, -clip, clip)
       if use_sym:
          h = sym_proj(h - step, n)
       else:
          h = project_feasible(h - step, n)
   best_c=compute_exact(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c
```
Potential issue: When stall random perturbs, we don't recompute r/grad immediately; next loop.
If deadline > current, while at least one iteration; if time check before first. Good.

random_smooth_start:
```
def random_smooth_start(n,rng,variant,use_sym=False):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
   h=np.full(n,0.5)
   K=5
   for mm in range(1,K+1):
      amp=0.35/(mm**1.2)*(0.3+0.7*rng.rand())
      ph=2*np.pi*rng.rand()
      h += amp*np.sin(np.pi*mm*x + ph)
      amp2=0.5*amp
      ph2=2*np.pi*rng.rand()
      h += amp2*np.cos(np.pi*mm*x + ph2)
   if variant % 2 == 0:
      mu=0.7+0.6*rng.rand()
      sig=0.25+0.5*rng.rand()
      amp=0.4+0.4*rng.rand()
      h += amp*np.exp(-((x-mu)**2)/(2*sig*sig))
   else:
      mu1=0.3+0.7*rng.rand(); mu2=0.4+0.7*rng.rand()
      sig=0.2+0.4*rng.rand(); amp=0.4*rng.rand()
      h += amp*(np.exp(-((x-mu1)**2)/(2*sig*sig)) + np.exp(-((x-mu2)**2)/(2*sig*sig)))*0.5
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
   return h
```
Could h sum off; project.

pair_delta/add_single:
```
def add_single_delta(delta_arr,idx,delta_val,h,n):
    s1=n-1-idx
    # first role k from -idx to n-1-idx: values 1-h[0:] over slice s1:s1+n
    delta_arr[s1:s1+n] += delta_val * (1.0 - h)
    s2=idx
    delta_arr[s2:s2+n] += -delta_val * h[::-1]
    delta_arr[n-1] -= delta_val*delta_val
```
Check if delta_arr length L; s1+s? s1=n-1-idx, s1+n=2n-1-idx <=2n-1 if idx>=0. s2+n=idx+n <=2n-1? if idx=n-1 =>2n-1. good.
```
def pair_delta(h,n,i,j,s):
    L=2*n-1
    darr=np.zeros(L)
    if s==0: return darr
    add_single_delta(darr,i,s,h,n)
    add_single_delta(darr,j,-s,h,n)
    if i != j:
       d=abs(i-j)
       corr=s*s
       darr[n-1+d] += corr
       darr[n-1-d] += corr
    return darr
```
For fractional zero correction: add_single subtracts s² and s². We tested. But wait for pair Δ_j=-s, Δ_j²=s². Good.

frac_anneal:
```
def frac_anneal(h0,n,deadline,seed,use_sym=False,T0=1.5):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   if use_sym: h=sym_proj(h,n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   current=float(c.max()); best=current; best_h=h.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   T=T0
   minT=1e-4
   F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0,True)
   if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
   step=0; since=0
   while time.time() < deadline:
      i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      if i==j: continue
      if rng.random()<0.75:
         diff=grad[i]-grad[j]
         if diff>1e-13: sign=-1.0
         elif diff<-1e-13: sign=1.0
         else: sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
      else:
         sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
      mag=0.005 + 0.04*rng.rand()
      s=sign*mag
      if s>0:
         maxs=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
         s=min(s,maxs)
      else:
         maxs=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
         s=max(s, -maxs)
      if abs(s)<1e-9: continue
      darr=pair_delta(h,n,i,j,s)
      c_trial=c+darr
      m_trial=float(c_trial.max())
      dE=m_trial-current
      accept=False
      if dE <= 1e-14:
         accept=True
         T=max(minT, 0.995*T)
      else:
         if math.log(1.0/(T+1e-300)) > dE: # or dE/T<50
            if rng.random() < math.exp(-dE/T): accept=True
      if accept:
         h[i]+=s; h[j]-=s; c=c_trial; current=m_trial
         step +=1
         since +=1
         if current < best - 1e-14:
            best=current; best_h=h.copy(); since=0
         if step % 100 ==0:
            F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0,True)
            if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
         if step % 2000 ==0:
            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            current=float(c.max())
         if since > 3000:
            h=best_h.copy()
            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            current=best
            since=0; step=0; T=T0
            F,r,weights,grad=smooth_obj_grad(h,n,1000.0,True)
            if use_sym: grad=0.5*(grad+grad[::-1])
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
   best_c=compute_exact(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c
```
math.log condition not needed; just if dE/T < 50. Use:

```
if dE < 50.0*T:
   if rng.random() < math.exp(-dE/T): accept=True
```
If T very small, dE<T? ok.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       parent=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_parent=len(parent)
    except Exception:
       parent=np.full(256,0.5); n_parent=256
    parent=project_feasible(parent,n_parent)
    best_h=parent.copy()
    best_c=compute_exact(parent,n_parent)
    # sym parent initial
    hs=sym_proj(parent,n_parent)
    cs=compute_exact(hs,n_parent)
    if cs < best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    # Phase parent free
    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
        alloc=min(150.0, max(50.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.18))
        hopt,copt=adam_optimize(parent,n_parent, min(deadline-30, time.time()+alloc), seed+1, False, 80,5000,0.06)
        if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    # sym parent
    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
        alloc=min(70.0, max(20.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.10))
        hopt,copt=adam_optimize(sym_proj(parent,n_parent),n_parent, min(deadline-40, time.time()+alloc), seed+2, True, 200,8000,0.04)
        if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    # Coarse
    n_coarse=64
    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
       rem=deadline - time.time() - 220
       starts=[interp_h(parent,n_parent,n_coarse), param_tent(n_coarse,1.0), param_cos_bump(n_coarse,2.0)]
       for vv in range(4):

       per=max(3.0, rem/float(len(starts)+1))
       per=min(15.0, per)
       for idx,st in enumerate(starts):
          if time.time() > deadline - 220: break
          if per <= 1.0: break
          u = True if (idx % 2 == 0 and n_coarse % 2 == 0) else False
          hopt,copt=adam_optimize(st,n_coarse, min(deadline-220, time.time()+per), seed+100+idx, u, 100,3000,0.10)
          if copt < best_c:
             best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
          best_h=np.asarray(best_h,float) # no
    # Fine/mid
    # But if best_c at coarse much lower? keep.
    fine_candidates=[]
    for cand in [512, 384, max(n_parent,128), 256, 192]:
       if cand not in fine_candidates and 32 <= cand <= 640:
          fine_candidates.append(cand)
    fine_candidates=sorted(fine_candidates)
    final_reserve=180.0
    for n_try in fine_candidates:
       if time.time() > deadline - final_reserve: break
       # If n_try close to current best len and not much improvement, maybe skip? Keep.
       if len(fine_candidates) > 2 and (len(fine_candidates)-1 - fine_candidates.index(n_try) > 1) and (deadline - time.time()) < 300:
           # only highest? but no index method? We can just break if remaining small.
           pass
       rem=deadline - time.time() - final_reserve
       if rem < 20: break
       starts=[]
       starts.append(interp_h(parent,n_parent,n_try))
       starts.append(interp_h(best_h,len(best_h),n_try))
       starts.append(param_tent(n_try,1.0))
       starts.append(param_cos_bump(n_try,2.0))
       # random 3
       for vv in range(3):
          starts.append(random_smooth_start(n_try,rng,2000+vv,use_sym=False))
       # maybe sym one
       if n_try % 2 == 0:
          starts.append(sym_proj(interp_h(best_h,len(best_h),n_try),n_try))
       per=rem/float(len(starts)+1)
       if n_try <= 256:
          per=max(15.0, min(50.0, per))
       elif n_try <= 384:
          per=max(25.0, min(65.0, per))
       else:
          per=max(35.0, min(85.0, per))
       for idx,st in enumerate(starts):
          if time.time() > deadline - final_reserve: break
          if per <= 3: break
          use_sym=False
          if idx == len(starts)-1: use_sym=True
          if n_try <= 256: alpha_hi=3000
          elif n_try <=384: alpha_hi=6000
          else: alpha_hi=8000
          hopt,copt=adam_optimize(st,n_try, min(deadline-final_reserve, time.time()+per), seed+5000+n_try+idx, use_sym, 200, alpha_hi, 0.05)
          if copt < best_c:
             best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    # Final polish
    n_best=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       rem=deadline - time.time() - 20
       sa_alloc=min(150.0, max(20.0, rem*0.45))
       if rem > 30 and n_best <= 512:
          hopt,copt=frac_anneal(best_h,n_best, time.time()+sa_alloc, seed+9000, False, 1.5)
          if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       rem=deadline - time.time() - 20
       ad_alloc=min(90.0, max(15.0, rem*0.35))
       hopt,copt=adam_optimize(best_h,len(best_h), time.time()+ad_alloc, seed+9500, False, 1000, 20000, 0.02, 0.0005)
       if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    # SLSQP if smaller
    n_best=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 30 and n_best <= 384:
       hopt,copt=slsqp_polish(best_h,n_best, min(60.0, (deadline-time.time()-30)*0.6), alpha=2500.0)
       if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    # final sym
    n_best=len(best_h)
    hs=sym_proj(best_h,n_best); cs=compute_exact(hs,n_best)
    if cs < best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    # short anneal if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       sa2=min(20.0, (deadline-time.time()-10)*0.7)
       hopt,copt=frac_anneal(best_h,n_best,time.time()+sa2,seed+9900,False,0.5)
       if copt < best_c: best_h,best_c=hopt,copt
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=compute_exact(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(12):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def sym_proj(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def compute_r(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    p = np.correlate(h, h, mode='full')
    L = 2 * n - 1
    r = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    total = float(h.sum())
    if n == 1:
        r[0] = dx * (total - p[0])
        return np.maximum(r, 0.0)

    prefix = np.cumsum(h)
    r[n - 1] = dx * (total - p[n - 1])

    d = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
    m = n - d
    pos_lin = prefix[m - 1]
    neg_lin = total - prefix[d - 1]
    pd = p[n - 1 + d]

    r[n - 1 + d] = dx * (pos_lin - pd)
    r[n - 1 - d] = dx * (neg_lin - pd)
    return np.maximum(r, 0.0)


def compute_exact(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)


def interp_h(hsrc, nsrc, ndst):
    hsrc = np.asarray(hsrc, dtype=float)
    if nsrc == ndst:
        return hsrc.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nsrc, endpoint=False)
    xd = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ndst, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(xd, xs, hsrc)


def add_lag_grad(grad, k, w, h, n):
    if w == 0.0:
        return
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cm = w * dx
    if k == 0:
        grad += cm * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    elif k > 0:
        d = k
        m = n - d
        if m > 0:
            grad[:m] += cm * (1.0 - h[d:])
            grad[d:] -= cm * h[:m]
    else:
        d = -k
        m = n + d
        if m > 0:
            grad[d:] += cm * (1.0 - h[:m])
            grad[:m] -= cm * h[d:]


def weighted_gradient(weights, h, n, allow_sparse=True):
    if n == 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)

    if allow_sparse:
        idx = np.argsort(weights)[::-1]
        cum = 0.0
        top = 0
        for ii in idx:
            w = weights[ii]
            if w <= 1e-12:
                break
            top += 1
            cum += w
            if cum >= 0.999:
                break
            if top >= 32:
                break

        if top <= 32 and cum >= 0.99:
            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for t in range(top):
                ii = int(idx[t])
                add_lag_grad(grad, ii - (n - 1), weights[ii], h, n)
            return grad

    w0 = weights[n - 1]
    pos = weights[n:]
    neg = weights[:n - 1][::-1]

    ar = np.arange(n)
    cpos = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos)))
    suffix_pos = cpos[n - 1 - ar]
    cneg = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(neg)))
    prefix_neg = cneg[ar]

    const = w0 + suffix_pos + prefix_neg

    wsum = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wsum[1:] = pos + neg

    direct = np.convolve(wsum, h, mode='full')
    rev = np.convolve(wsum, h[::-1], mode='full')

    term_past = direct[:n]
    term_future = rev[:n][::-1]

    grad_un = const - 2.0 * w0 * h - term_past - term_future
    return (2.0 / float(n)) * grad_un


def smooth_obj_grad(h, n, alpha, allow_sparse=True):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        alpha = 1.0

    r = compute_r(h, n)
    m = float(r.max())
    scaled = alpha * (r - m)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    sumexp = float(exps.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(sumexp) or sumexp <= 0.0:
        exps = np.zeros_like(r)
        exps[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
        sumexp = 1.0

    weights = exps / sumexp
    fval = (m + math.log(sumexp)) / alpha
    grad = weighted_gradient(weights, h, n, allow_sparse)
    return fval, r, weights, grad


def add_single_delta(delta_arr, idx, delta_val, h, n):
    s1 = n - 1 - idx
    delta_arr[s1:s1 + n] += delta_val * (1.0 - h)
    s2 = idx
    delta_arr[s2:s2 + n] += -delta_val * h[::-1]
    delta_arr[n - 1] -= delta_val * delta_val


def pair_delta(h, n, i, j, s):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    darr = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if s == 0.0:
        return darr
    add_single_delta(darr, i, s, h, n)
    add_single_delta(darr, j, -s, h, n)
    if i != j:
        d = abs(i - j)
        corr = s * s
        darr[n - 1 + d] += corr
        darr[n - 1 - d] += corr
    return darr


def adam_optimize(h0, n, deadline, seed, use_sym=False,
                  alpha_lo=80.0, alpha_hi=4000.0, lr0=0.06, lr1=0.002):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    if use_sym:
        h = sym_proj(h, n)

    best_c = compute_exact(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    t = 0
    stall = 0
    clip = 0.10

    while time.time() < deadline:
        frac = min(1.0, t / 800.0)
        alpha = alpha_lo + (alpha_hi - alpha_lo) * frac

        _, r, _, grad = smooth_obj_grad(h, n, alpha, True)
        if use_sym:
            grad = 0.5 * (grad + grad[::-1])

        exact = float(r.max())
        if exact < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c = exact
            best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        t += 1
        lr = lr0 * (lr1 / lr0) ** (min(1.0, t / 1500.0))

        if stall > 400:
            h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            if use_sym:
                h = sym_proj(h, n)
            else:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            stall = 0

        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * grad
        v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (grad * grad)

        b1 = 1.0 - 0.9 ** t
        b2 = 1.0 - 0.999 ** t
        step = lr * (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -clip, clip)

        if use_sym:
            h = sym_proj(h - step, n)
        else:
            h = project_feasible(h - step, n)

    best_c = compute_exact(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def param_tent(n, exponent=1.0, center=1.0, width=1.0, height=1.2):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    t = 1.0 - np.abs(x - center) / max(1e-12, float(width))
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    h = height * (t ** exponent)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def param_cos_bump(n, exponent=2.0, center=1.0):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    dist = np.abs(x - center)
    a = np.cos(0.5 * math.pi * dist)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = a ** exponent
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def random_smooth_start(n, rng, variant, use_sym=False):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    K = 5
    for mm in range(1, K + 1):
        amp = 0.35 / (float(mm) ** 1.2) * (0.3 + 0.7 * rng.rand())
        ph = 2.0 * math.pi * rng.rand()
        h += amp * np.sin(math.pi * float(mm) * x + ph)

        amp2 = 0.5 * amp
        ph2 = 2.0 * math.pi * rng.rand()
        h += amp2 * np.cos(math.pi * float(mm) * x + ph2)

    if variant % 2 == 0:
        mu = 0.7 + 0.6 * rng.rand()
        sig = 0.25 + 0.5 * rng.rand()
        amp = 0.4 + 0.4 * rng.rand()
        h += amp * np.exp(-((x - mu) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    else:
        mu1 = 0.3 + 0.7 * rng.rand()
        mu2 = 0.4 + 0.7 * rng.rand()
        sig = 0.2 + 0.4 * rng.rand()
        amp = 0.4 * rng.rand()
        h += 0.5 * amp * (
            np.exp(-((x - mu1) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig)) +
            np.exp(-((x - mu2) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        )

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if use_sym:
        h = sym_proj(h, n)
    return h


def frac_anneal(h0, n, deadline, seed, use_sym=False, T0=1.5):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    if use_sym:
        h = sym_proj(h, n)

    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    current = float(c.max())
    best = current
    best_h = h.copy()

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T = T0
    minT = 1e-4

    _, _, _, grad = smooth_obj_grad(h, n, 1000.0, True)
    if use_sym:
        grad = 0.5 * (grad + grad[::-1])

    step = 0
    since_best = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        i = rng.randint(n)
        j = rng.randint(n)
        if i == j:
            continue

        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            diff = grad[i] - grad[j]
            if diff > 1e-13:
                sign = -1.0
            elif diff < -1e-13:
                sign = 1.0
            else:
                sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        else:
            sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0

        mag = 0.005 + 0.04 * rng.rand()
        s = sign * mag

        if s > 0.0:
            maxs = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            s = min(s, maxs)
        else:
            maxs = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            s = max(s, -maxs)

        if abs(s) < 1e-9:
            continue

        darr = pair_delta(h, n, i, j, s)
        c_trial = c + darr
        m_trial = float(c_trial.max())
        dE = m_trial - current

        accept = False
        if dE <= 1e-14:
            accept = True
            T = max(minT, 0.995 * T)
        else:
            if dE < 50.0 * T:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-dE / T):
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            h[i] += s
            h[j] -= s
            c = c_trial
            current = m_trial
            step += 1
            since_best += 1

            if current < best - 1e-14:
                best = current
                best_h = h.copy()
                since_best = 0

            if step % 100 == 0:
                _, _, _, grad = smooth_obj_grad(h, n, 1000.0, True)
                if use_sym:
                    grad = 0.5 * (grad + grad[::-1])

            if step % 2000 == 0:
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                current = float(c.max())

            if since_best > 3000:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                current = best
                since_best = 0
                step = 0
                T = T0
                _, _, _, grad = smooth_obj_grad(h, n, 1000.0, True)
                if use_sym:
                    grad = 0.5 * (grad + grad[::-1])

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_exact(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def _sum_con(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n))


def _sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def _slsqp_obj(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    return float(smooth_obj_grad(x, n, alpha, False)[0])


def _slsqp_jac(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    return smooth_obj_grad(x, n, alpha, False)[3]


def slsqp_polish(h0, n, budget_s, alpha=2500.0):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c0 = compute_exact(h, n)
    if budget_s < 15.0:
        return h, c0

    if n <= 200:
        maxiter = 160
    elif n <= 300:
        maxiter = 100
    else:
        maxiter = 50

    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _slsqp_obj,
            h,
            args=(n, alpha),
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=_slsqp_jac,
            options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12}
        )
        if hasattr(res, 'x'):
            hp = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
            cp = compute_exact(hp, n)
            if cp < c0:
                return hp, cp
    except Exception:
        pass

    return h, c0


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_parent = len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.full(256, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n_parent = 256

    parent = project_feasible(parent, n_parent)
    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c = compute_exact(parent, n_parent)

    hp = sym_proj(parent, n_parent)
    cp = compute_exact(hp, n_parent)
    if cp < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hp, cp

    # Polish the supplied parent in its native resolution.
    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
        alloc = min(150.0, max(50.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.18))
        hopt, copt = adam_optimize(
            parent, n_parent,
            min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + alloc),
            seed + 1, False, 80.0, 5000.0, 0.06, 0.002
        )
        if copt < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
        alloc = min(70.0, max(20.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.10))
        hopt, copt = adam_optimize(
            sym_proj(parent, n_parent), n_parent,
            min(deadline - 40.0, time.time() + alloc),
            seed + 2, True, 200.0, 8000.0, 0.04, 0.001
        )
        if copt < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    # Coarse quick exploration to escape local basins.
    n_coarse = 64
    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
        rem = deadline - time.time() - 220.0
        starts = [
            interp_h(parent, n_parent, n_coarse),
            param_tent(n_coarse, 1.0),
            param_cos_bump(n_coarse, 2.0),
        ]
        for vv in range(4):
            starts.append(random_smooth_start(n_coarse, rng, 10 + vv, use_sym=(vv % 2 == 0)))

        per = max(3.0, rem / float(len(starts) + 1))
        per = min(15.0, per)

        for idx, st in enumerate(starts):
            if time.time() > deadline - 220:
                break
            if per <= 1.0:
                break
            use_sym = (idx % 2 == 0 and n_coarse % 2 == 0)
            hopt, copt = adam_optimize(
                st, n_coarse,
                min(deadline - 220.0, time.time() + per),
                seed + 100 + idx, use_sym, 100.0, 3000.0, 0.10, 0.005
            )
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    # Medium/fine resolution optimization.
    base = max(n_parent, 160)
    priority = []
    if base != 384 and base != 512:
        priority.append(base)
    if 384 not in priority:
        priority.append(384)
    if 512 not in priority:
        priority.append(512)

    fine_candidates = []
    for cand in priority:
        if 32 <= cand <= 640 and cand not in fine_candidates:
            fine_candidates.append(cand)
    fine_candidates = fine_candidates[:3]

    final_reserve = 180.0
    for n_try in fine_candidates:
        if time.time() > deadline - final_reserve:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time() - final_reserve
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        if n_try <= 256:
            max_starts = 4
            max_per = 45.0
            alpha_hi = 3000.0
        elif n_try <= 384:
            max_starts = 4
            max_per = 60.0
            alpha_hi = 6000.0
        else:
            max_starts = 3
            max_per = 75.0
            alpha_hi = 8000.0

        starts = [
            interp_h(parent, n_parent, n_try),
            interp_h(best_h, len(best_h), n_try),
            param_tent(n_try, 1.0),
            param_cos_bump(n_try, 2.0),
        ]
        for vv in range(3):
            starts.append(random_smooth_start(n_try, rng, 2000 + vv, use_sym=False))
        if n_try % 2 == 0:
            starts.append(sym_proj(interp_h(best_h, len(best_h), n_try), n_try))

        if len(starts) > max_starts:
            starts = starts[:max_starts]

        per = rem / float(len(starts) + 1)
        per = min(max_per, per)
        if per < 12.0:
            break

        for idx, st in enumerate(starts):
            if time.time() > deadline - final_reserve:
                break
            if per <= 3.0:
                break
            use_sym = (idx == len(starts) - 1 and n_try % 2 == 0)
            hopt, copt = adam_optimize(
                st, n_try,
                min(deadline - final_reserve, time.time() + per),
                seed + 5000 + n_try + idx,
                use_sym,
                200.0, alpha_hi, 0.05, 0.001
            )
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    # Final local refinements.
    n_best = len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        rem = deadline - time.time() - 25.0
        sa_alloc = min(150.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.45))
        if rem > 35.0 and n_best <= 512:
            hopt, copt = frac_anneal(
                best_h, n_best,
                time.time() + sa_alloc,
                seed + 9000, False, 1.5
            )
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        rem = deadline - time.time() - 25.0
        ad_alloc = min(90.0, max(15.0, rem * 0.35))
        hopt, copt = adam_optimize(
            best_h, len(best_h),
            time.time() + ad_alloc,
            seed + 9500, False, 1000.0, 20000.0, 0.02, 0.0005
        )
        if copt < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    n_best = len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 35 and n_best <= 384:
        hopt, copt = slsqp_polish(
            best_h, n_best,
            min(60.0, (deadline - time.time() - 35.0) * 0.6),
            alpha=2500.0
        )
        if copt < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    n_best = len(best_h)
    hs = sym_proj(best_h, n_best)
    cs = compute_exact(hs, n_best)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    n_best = len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 12:
        sa2 = min(20.0, max(5.0, (deadline - time.time() - 12.0) * 0.7))
        hopt, copt = frac_anneal(
            best_h, n_best,
            time.time() + sa2,
            seed + 9900, False, 0.5
        )
        if copt < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hopt, copt

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_exact(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```
